## Intimate Feline Companion Portrait

> Minimal, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an image features a close-up portrait of a young woman with dark hair and a nose ring, wearing a {argument name="clothing" default="teal and gold patterned headscarf"}. She has a gentle expression and is holding a {argument name="companion" default="fluffy, grey cat"} close to her face. The cat appears somewhat unimpressed or sleepy, with its amber eyes narrowed toward the camera. The photo has a nostalgic, film-like quality characterized by warm skin tones and a soft, slightly grainy texture. The background is simple and dark, which allows the focus to remain entirely on the bond between the woman and her feline companion. The overall composition feels intimate and candid, capturing a quiet, domestic moment. Without flower in hair please.
```

## Striped Window Shadow Editorial

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyper-photorealistic portrait of the same female subject, preserving exact face, skin tone, and proportions.

Scene:
Subject standing near a window with striped shadow patterns crossing her face, neck, and chest, wearing a {argument name="outfit" default="chiffon lace white button-up sleeveless"}, wind moving hair across her lips, dreamy editorial mood.

Camera settings:
Early-2000s CCD digital camera with direct flash softened by daylight, visible grain, slight color inconsistency, bright highlights on nose and lips, imperfect handheld framing. Portra 400 color rendering: warm creamy skin, pastel whites, muted saturation, gentle highlight roll-off layered over flash harshness, neutral-to-warm white balance.

Makeup:
Rosy complexion, dewy with visible pores, brushed-up feathery brows, thin wing eyeliner, wispy eyelashes, grey contact lenses, pink lip gloss.

Hair:
{argument name="hair style" default="Modern long layered style with inward upward curled ends"}, messy windswept motion, loose strands crossing the face, airy volume.

Negative prompt:
no overly clean studio photo, no plastic texture, no face blur, no heavy film filter.
```

## Moody Editorial CCD Digicam Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create image: A hyper-photorealistic close-up portrait of the same female subject, preserving exact facial identity, skin tone, and proportions.

Scene:
Subject leaning close against a {argument name="background" default="plain dark grey wall"}, messy hair blowing across her face, one shoulder slightly forward, intense soft gaze into camera, {argument name="atmosphere" default="moody editorial bedroom atmosphere"}.

Camera settings:
Strong CCD digicam aesthetic: direct flash, harsh shadow behind subject, bright facial highlights, visible grain, slight color inconsistency. Portra 400 skin rendering layered: warm balanced tones, creamy highlights, muted saturation. Shot on an {argument name="camera type" default="early-2000s CCD digital camera"}, direct on-camera flash, harsh flash falloff, bright hotspot on face and collarbone, slightly overexposed highlights, visible digital grain, imperfect snapshot framing. Color rendering inspired by Kodak Portra 400: warm creamy skin tones, pastel softness, muted saturation, gentle highlight roll-off layered over flash harshness. Neutral-to-warm white balance.

Makeup:
Rosy complexion, dewy skin with visible pores, brushed-up feathery brows, thin wing eyeliner, wispy eyelashes, grey contact lenses, pink lip gloss.

Hair:
Modern layered hairstyle with inward upward curled ends, messy windswept movement, face-framing strands crossing the eyes and cheeks, airy volume at the crown.

Negative prompt:
no plastic skin, no over-smoothing, no face change, no HDR, no teal-orange grading, no overly perfect studio lighting.
```

## Tropical Beach Palm Bridge

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Insert your appearance parameters: {argument name="age" default="25 years old"}, {argument name="hair" default="long wavy dark hair"}, {argument name="body build" default="athletic yet feminine figure"}, no beard]

Hyper-realistic vertical photograph 4:5. Tropical beach with a turquoise lagoon stretching to the horizon. In the center of the frame — a single coconut palm with a horizontally curved trunk extending over the water, forming a natural bridge. A young woman is lying relaxed along the trunk in a graceful pose, head resting on one hand, fingers gently touching the surface of the water.

Clothing: olive-green sleeveless tank top, dark gray tailored Bermuda shorts, khaki-beige bucket hat pushed back on her head, delicate silver chain with a small cross pendant, elegant steel Patek Philippe Aquanaut watch with blue dial. Bare feet, with a discarded suede clog nearby.

Lighting: golden hour, warm sunset light, backlit, soft golden highlights on the palm bark and water, emerald-aquamarine accents.

Camera: Leica M11, 50mm f/1.4 lens, 8K sharpness, natural skin and bark texture, natural depth of field. No plasticity, CGI, or AI artifacts.
```

## Cinematic Urban Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the uploaded reference image as the {argument name="facial identity" default="facial identity base"}. Preserve exact facial structure, skin tone, hairstyle (slightly wavy, styled with loose strands falling on the forehead), trimmed beard, and sharp jawline. Maintain a realistic, masculine appearance with natural skin texture and no over-smoothing. A cinematic, ultra-realistic portrait of the same {argument name="subject" default="young man"} standing in a {argument name="environment" default="crowded urban street"} during golden hour. Shot in a side-profile (3/4 angle), mid-close framing. His expression is calm, introspective, slightly distant—eyes softly focused ahead as if lost in thought. Lighting & Atmosphere: Strong golden-hour sunlight from behind creates a dramatic rim light halo around his hair and shoulders. Individual hair strands glow with warm highlights. Subtle wind moves his hair naturally. Add cinematic lens flares, soft light leaks, and atmospheric haze. Wardrobe: Replace formal attire with a casual modern outfit: fitted dark t-shirt or lightweight jacket, visible backpack strap over one shoulder, fabric subtly catching warm sunlight. Environment: Busy urban street scene with motion-blurred pedestrians in the background. The crowd is out of focus, creating separation and depth. Background filled with warm tones—amber, orange, gold—with darker silhouettes for contrast. Camera & Technicals: Shot on 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, shallow depth of field, creamy cinematic bokeh, subject sharply in focus, background heavily blurred. Color & Grade: Kodak Portra-style color grading, warm highlights, soft shadows, high dynamic range, subtle film grain for realism. Mood: Cinematic, emotional, introspective, slightly nostalgic, urban solitude. Style Tags: ultra-realistic, cinematic lighting, volumetric light, backlit portrait, golden hour, lens flare, shallow depth of field, 8k, photorealistic, film still
```

Preserve exactly:
Facial structure & bone anatomy
Jawline, cheekbones, chin
Eye shape, spacing, gaze
Nose, lips, brow shape
Skin tone & natural texture
Hairline, hairstyle, density
❌ No beautification

❌ No stylization

❌ No face reshaping

❌ No identity drift
Scene & Composition
Shot from outside a {argument name="setting" default="glass café at night"}.

The main subject is centered inside the café, standing still and facing the camera.
In the foreground, a dark shadowy person walks past, rendered with strong horizontal motion blur, partially blocking the frame and creating depth.

Only a narrow clear area reveals the subject sharply.
Subject
Young adult.

Expression: {argument name="expression" default="natural, calm, slightly sad (not forced)"}.

Right hand holding a disposable coffee cup at chest height, left hand relaxed.
Outfit
{argument name="clothing" default="Oversized dark sweatshirt"}

Long white T-shirt underneath

Loose beige trousers

White sneakers
Environment
A warm night café with plants: hanging vines, tall grass-like plants, wooden tables, soft fairy lights, gentle reflections on the glass.

Outside remains dark with soft city bokeh.
Lighting & Mood
Low-key cinematic lighting.

Most of the scene is dark, with a soft warm glow around the subject only.

Subtle contrast, film-style color grading, realistic shadows.
Camera Style
Eye-level, full-body framing, shallow depth of field.

Foreground motion blur, subject in sharp focus.

Natural film grain, 4K realism.

Create image: Use the attached reference image as a visual guide for the character’s appearance, maintaining strong resemblance to the person in the reference. Preserve key visual traits such as facial structure and overall proportions, while allowing small natural variations typical of real photography. The generated subject should clearly resemble the reference while remaining a natural photographic interpretation.  **Scene & Style Paragraph** The scene is set against a {argument name="background" default="bustling urban backdrop at twilight, where towering concrete structures and glowing streetlights create a sense of late-night city energy"}. The photography captures a classic early-2000s digital aesthetic, characterized by a harsh direct flash that sharply illuminates the subject while casting deep, dramatic shadows behind her. A fine layer of low-fidelity grain covers the image, mimicking the output of a vintage point-and-shoot camera. The color palette is dominated by cool city blues and stark whites, contrasted by the warm, amber glow of distant office windows and traffic signals, giving the entire composition a spontaneous, lo-fi cinematic grade.  **Pose & Expression** The subject embodies a candid, high-energy Y2K persona. She is captured in a mid-motion stance, with her head tilted playfully toward her shoulder and a soft, magnetic gaze directed at the lens. Her right arm is raised, with fingers delicately threaded through her long, raven-colored hair as if caught in a spontaneous adjustment. Her left hand firmly grips the handle of a heavy tote bag, while her torso is slightly arched to emphasize the silhouette of her outfit. The expression is one of effortless confidence, featuring a slight pout and a youthful, fresh-faced glow that enhances the snapshot's authentic, unposed feel.  **Fashion & Accessories** The outfit is a masterclass in turn-of-the-millennium street style. She wears a {argument name="outfit" default="navy and white horizontally striped long-sleeved polo shirt, crafted from a structured cotton piqué"}. The shirt features a crisp white collar and a prominent, intricately embroidered heraldic crest on the chest in shades of gold, emerald, and cerulean. The garment is cut in an ultra-cropped style, leaving the midriff uncovered to meet a pair of low-slung, light-wash denim jeans with a relaxed, straight-leg fit. Hanging from her hand is a large, black leather moto-style bag, adorned with burnished silver studs, long leather tassels, and heavy metallic buckles. Her long, straight hair cascades down her back, and her makeup is finished with a dewy, high-shine lip gloss and a soft pink flush across the cheekbones, completing the quintessential Y2K aesthetic.    Negative: {argument name="negative prompt" default="extra limbs, extra hands, extra fingers, missing fingers, fused fingers, dislocated hands, broken wrists, unnatural hand pose, twisted arms, duplicated body parts, bad anatomy, incorrect anatomy, broken anatomy, impossible pose, warped body, distorted proportions, ai ar"}

## Portrait Master Object: String Phone Passport Photos

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

Create a Portrait Master Object: Two passport-sized photo prints held side-by-side by a person's thumb and index finger.
**Passport Photo Background:** Use a solid blue background (the standard official passport photo color for Indonesian marriage books for certain even/odd birth years).
**Clothing:** Both subjects in the photo wear formal and neat white collared shirts.
**Male Subject (Left Photo):** Make his face and hair 100% similar to the given reference.
He poses as if talking into a white paper cup, with a cheerful laughing expression.
**Female Subject (Right Photo):** Make her face and hair 100% similar to the given reference.
She poses as if holding a white paper cup to her ear, listening. Her expression is funny with a wide smile and a slightly stuck-out tongue.
**Interactive Prop:** There is a white string coming out from under the male's paper cup and connecting to the female's paper cup. Because the two photos are placed next to each other, this prop creates the illusion of a "string phone" connecting the two photos.
```

A cinematic low-angle portrait shot on a 50mm lens, looking upward at a male subject using the uploaded face as reference. He leans casually against a weathered concrete and metal railing, resting both elbows. The subject has short dark hair with subtle sun-kissed highlights and light natural texture, with a slightly tousled appearance. He wears an oversized, textured beige long-sleeve crewneck sweatshirt with a relaxed, loose drape.
He gazes upward to the right with a calm, introspective expression, conveying quiet confidence and contemplation.
Soft, warm natural sunlight illuminates the scene, casting gentle shadows along the jawline and emphasizing facial structure with realistic skin subsurface scattering. A strong foreground bokeh from a structural element on the right adds depth and framing.
The background is a vast, solid deep teal-cyan sky, creating a cinematic teal-and-orange analog film color grade, with subtle film grain and natural contrast.
```

## Day and Night Split Portrait Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Day and night split portrait:

A hyper-realistic 8K photo of a young woman's portrait, with her face split vertically and sharply down the center of her nose, representing two different times of day. The LEFT HALF of her face is illuminated by warm, golden afternoon daylight, with sun-kissed skin and eye reflections of the sun and clouds. Her hair shows warm highlights, and she wears a white linen shirt on the left shoulder. The background behind the left side features a bright blue sky, a sunflower, green leaves, and a butterfly near her left ear. The RIGHT HALF of her face is bathed in cool, blue moonlight, with a cool-toned, slightly silvery complexion, and eye reflections of moonlight and stars. Her hair shows cool blue highlights, and she wears a dark velvet dress visible on the right shoulder. The background on the right shows a dark night sky with visible stars, a crescent moon, fireflies glowing, and a moth near her right ear. The transition between the two halves is abrupt and sharp, with a perfect vertical dividing line running through the center of the nose bridge, creating a stunning and impossible juxtaposition of day and night. The woman maintains a calm, direct gaze with the same expression on both halves. The lighting setup is distinctly different on each side, with warm 3200K daylight from the left and cool 7000K moonlight from the right, with no bleed. The image is captured on an 85mm f/1.8 lens as a head-and-shoulders close-up at direct eye-level angle, with a shallow depth of field, rendering a photorealistic, clean, and beautiful image with share-worthy qualities.
```

## 8K Stylized Digital Portrait with Identity Lock

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an 8k high-detail stylized digital portrait illustration of a person with a slightly happy, gently uplifted expression, maintaining realistic facial structure and natural imperfections, captured at an eye-level camera angle with the subject looking directly into the camera.
Use the uploaded image as the primary identity reference.
Face Accuracy (Critical)
Match the face with 100% identity accuracy. Preserve exact facial structure, bone anatomy, grooming details, jawline, cheekbones, and forehead proportions.
Subject & Expression:
The person has natural eyes with visible iris details. His face shows noticeable forehead lines. His expression feels subtly warm and introspective, with a faint, relaxed softness around the eyes and a very slight, natural hint of a smile — not exaggerated, just a mild uplift in mood while preserving realism.
Hair:
Natural-length, natural colour hair, slightly naturally messy, with textured strands highlighted in lighter colour tones to add depth and volume.
Outfit:
He wears a dark green and brown plaid (tartan) jacket with a loose, painterly pattern. Underneath is a muted beige/tan button-down shirt with a neatly tucked collar. The clothing should feel grounded, simple, and slightly worn.
Composition & Framing (IMPORTANT):
Head-and-shoulders portrait only (no full body)
Subject perfectly centered in the frame
Shoulders must NOT touch the edges — maintain clean breathing space
Bottom of the artwork should fade into a soft, rough, painterly edge (subtle cut-out feel)
Negative space below the portrait to draw focus upward to the face
Background:
Minimal cream background. Behind the head, place a bold orange square with slightly rough, painterly edges to create contrast and a halo-like framing effect.
Lighting:
Soft directional lighting from the upper left, creating gentle highlights on the forehead, nose bridge, and cheek, with subtle shadows on the opposite side to enhance facial structure and depth.
Style & Rendering:
Semi-realistic illustration blending editorial portrait art and graphic novel style
Strong sketch-like linework with expressive strokes
Visible textured brushwork and painterly shading
Dry brush effects, especially around the shoulders, where the image softly dissolves
Natural skin texture with varied tones (subtle reds, neutral hues, faint bluish undertones for stubble effect)
No beautification — preserve raw realism and imperfections
Quality Tags:
ultra-detailed, 8k high resolution, sharp focus, textured brush strokes, cinematic portrait, centered composition, premium illustration style
Aspect ratio 4:5
```

## Springtime Cherry Blossom Portrait Recreation Prompt

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Recreate a springtime portrait similar to the reference image. A young woman stands in a park surrounded by blooming cherry blossom trees, holding a branch of pink flowers. Ensure the facial features of the young woman are 100% exact to the reference image; do not alter the face. Use soft, diffused natural light, as if taken on an overcast day or during golden hour. Employ a medium telephoto lens (50-85mm) with an aperture around f/2.8 to f/4 to achieve a shallow depth of field. The subject should be in the lower center of the frame, with the blossoms framing the top and sides. Capture a calm, serene, and fresh atmosphere, emphasizing the beauty of spring. Apply subtle post-processing to enhance brightness, contrast, and color saturation, with a warm color grading and slightly desaturated tones. The final image should be high-quality and authentic, avoiding overly stylized effects.
```

## Modern Lifestyle Mirror Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic 4:5 modern lifestyle mirror selfie with a fashion photography aesthetic. Use the reference image for the subject’s identity, face, and hair — preserve exact facial features, proportions, and likeness.

The subject is sitting casually on the floor, taking a mirror selfie. Her right arm is extended behind her with her palm flat on the floor for support, while her left hand holds a smartphone near her face. Her right leg is bent inward on the floor, and her left leg is raised with the foot flat on the ground.

She is wearing a glossy black strapless tube top made of a reflective material (latex or faux leather), paired with medium-wash blue denim jeans in a relaxed fit. She also wears black pointed-toe stiletto ankle boots.

The setting is an empty modern high-rise apartment or office space. The floor is smooth, light grey polished concrete with subtle reflections, and the walls are plain white. A large floor mirror is visible along the right edge of the frame.

In the background, three tall rectangular windows with metallic frames reveal a bright daytime cityscape with glass skyscrapers and a clear blue sky.

Lighting is bright natural daylight streaming in from the windows, creating soft backlighting, gentle shadows on the floor, and even illumination on the subject.

High detail, sharp focus, realistic textures, clean modern aesthetic, shallow depth of field.
```

## Ultra-Clean Realistic Human and Stylized 3D Caricature Composition Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Proportions, skin tone, hairstyle, and all recognizable features. Do not alter or stylize the real face.
Composition & Framing:

Full-body vertical shot with a clean, centered layout. The real subject stands on the right, and the caricature stands on the left at approximately 50–60% of the real subject’s height. The real subject casually rests one elbow on the caricature’s oversized head, creating a natural, playful interaction.
Real Subject (Photographic):

Ultra-realistic rendering with natural skin texture and fine detail. Expression is warm, relaxed, and confident.
Pose & Style (IMPORTANT):

The subject stands in a slightly cool, stylish posture — relaxed stance with subtle asymmetry (one leg slightly forward or weight shifted), shoulders loose, not stiff. The hand resting on the caricature should feel effortless and natural.
Clothing (Match Reference):

{argument name="clothing" default="Gray crewneck sweater (fine knit texture, ribbed cuffs/hem), classic blue jeans (straight fit), and clean white sneakers"}. Keep textures realistic and clearly visible.
3D Caricature (Stylized Version):

Pixar/Disney-inspired 3D style with exaggerated proportions — large expressive head and smaller body. The height should be noticeably shorter, around the real subject's waist level.
Facial features must remain recognizable but enhanced — larger eyes, softer forms, slightly amplified smile.
Emotion & Body Movement (IMPORTANT):

Expression is more playful and animated than the real subject

Eyes brighter and more expressive, smile slightly exaggerated
Body posture must NOT be stiff — use a natural, lively stance

Add subtle movement according to the real subject's personality

Pose should feel like a living, animated character, not a rigid figurine
Clothing:

Exact 1:1 stylized recreation of the real subject’s outfit with soft 3D textures.
Lighting & Environment:

Soft, diffused studio lighting from the upper-left, creating smooth highlights and gentle shadows. Clean light-gray gradient seamless background(lighter in the inner areas).
Output Style:

Ultra-clean, cinematic, high-detail render with strong contrast between realistic human and stylized 3D character. Both subjects grounded with soft natural shadows.
```

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(9:16) Raw high-fidelity mirror selfie, eye-level, medium shot, iPhone 17 Pro sim, 35mm, f/2.8, sharp subject, natural grain.

Framing: Subject ~65% frame, centered in mirror, large wooden door background, slight white wall edge, ~0.6m distance.

Identity Lock: Preserve ALL facial features (structure, skin, hair, marks).
Enhancement: Natural skin clarity (visible pores), even tone, soft glow, subtle light-based contour, refined highlights (eyes/lashes/brows), salon hair.
Bio-Fidelity: Micro-pores, fine vellus hair, hydrated skin.
Expression: Confident/poised gaze into phone via mirror, lower face partially occluded by phone.

Hair: Same color/texture, long sleek waves, middle part, behind shoulders + over left shoulder, warm highlights.
Makeup: Polished (filled brows, eyeliner, mascara), neutral nails.

Accessories: Gold watch (left), layered gold necklaces, gold ring (right), beige/gold iPhone, small off-white handbag (left hand).

Outfit: Strapless rib-knit bodycon dress, abstract marble (charcoal/olive/cream), tight fit, realistic folds, defined S-curve.

Pose: Torso angled ~30° left, hips toward mirror, right hand holding phone at face level, left holding bag, natural articulation.

Mood/Color: Chic, confident; palette (mahogany, charcoal, olive, cream, gold), ~3500K warm indoor light, moderate contrast.

Environment: Indoor dressing/hallway, mahogany door w/ silver handle, white wall edge, soft overhead lighting, all sharp.
```

## Hyper-realistic iPhone Photo Prompt (Gemini Nano Banana)

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create image: Use the attached reference image as a visual guide for the character’s appearance, maintaining strong resemblance to the person in the reference. Preserve key visual traits such as facial structure and overall proportions, while allowing small natural variations typical of real photography. The generated subject should clearly resemble the reference while remaining a natural photographic interpretation.
Aspect Ratio: 3:4.
A hyper-realistic, ultra-sharp photograph taken on an iPhone 15 Pro, characterized by digital clarity. Shot on a wide-angle lens, ISO 50, f/1.8. A young woman is captured mid-moment, angled 20 degrees right with a natural weight shift and one shoulder subtly lowered. Her right hand is raised near the shoulder, fingers relaxed with long white nails. She features a voluminous black wolf cut with shaggy layers and face-framing hime-style strands. Her expression is poised but intrigued; intense blue eyes stare through rimless geometric glasses while her left eyebrow shows a slight micro-arch. Her makeup is a pink 'clean girl' style with winged liner, nose blush, and blurred matte lips. Skin shows a healthy radiance with visible pores and a dewy glass finish. She wears a baby pink ribbed knit top with small pearl buttons and separate arm sleeves. Illuminated by soft, diffused front-side lighting with gentle jawline shadows. The scene is in sharp focus from fabric textures to the dark background, devoid of bokeh, with realistic digital noise.
```

## Hyper-Realistic Smartphone Selfie Prompt for Gemini Nano Banana

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create image: 8k UHD, slightly desaturated color palette. Use the attached reference image as a visual guide for the character’s appearance. Preserve key visual traits such as facial structure and overall proportions, while allowing small natural variations typical of real photography. The generated subject should clearly resemble the reference while remaining a natural photographic interpretation.
Aspect Ratio: 3:4.
A hyper-realistic, ultra-sharp photograph taken on a modern smartphone, specifically an iPhone 15 Pro, characterized by its digital clarity. Shot on a modern high-end smartphone camera, wide-angle lens, ISO 50, f/1.8. The subject is a young woman in her early 20s, captured in a spontaneous, wide-angle selfie pose at the Piazza del Duomo in Pisa. Her right arm is extended forward toward the lens, creating a prominent foreshortened perspective, while her torso is angled 20 degrees to the left with her right shoulder naturally elevated. She has ash-blonde hair styled in shaggy layers with soft curtain bangs that appear slightly wind-tossed. Her expression is radiant and beaming, layered with a subtle nose scrunch from the bright sun; her eyes are partially visible behind dark brown tortoiseshell oval sunglasses, showing a joyful squint. Her skin exhibits a healthy radiance and a rosy warmth, featuring visible pores and a clear, dewy texture with soft highlights on the bridge and tip of the nose. She wears glossy, natural pinkish-mauve lips with a subtle shimmer and a subtle blush applied with high placement on the cheekbones. She is dressed in a crisp, white linen button-down shirt with a visible breast pocket and slightly wrinkled fabric texture, paired with a stack of various cream-colored beaded and pearl bracelets on her extended wrist. A large white canvas tote bag with thick straps is slung over her left shoulder. The background features the Leaning Tower of Pisa in microscopic detail, showing the intricate marble arches and weathered stone textures against a vibrant blue sky with scattered wispy clouds. The entire scene is rendered in incredibly sharp focus, from the foreground bracelets to the distant green hedges and white information kiosks, without any background blur or bokeh. The lighting is characterized by soft daylight that enhances the rosy skin tones and preserves a natural, low-contrast digital texture devoid of film grain.
```

## iPhone Pro Environmental Portrait on Terrace

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(9:16) Raw high-fidelity photo, eye-level, medium-wide environmental portrait, iPhone 17 Pro sim, 35mm, f/2.8, natural bokeh, authentic grain.

Framing: Subject ~60% frame height, camera ~2m, outdoor terrace, wide horizon sky (top ~40%), glass railings, minimal foreground clutter.

Identity Lock: Preserve ALL facial features (structure, skin, hair, marks).
Enhancement: Natural skin clarity (pores visible), even tone, soft glow, subtle light-based contour, refined highlights (eyes/lips/cheekbones), natural lashes/brows/teeth, salon hair.
Bio-Fidelity: Micro-pores, fine vellus hair, natural skin hydration.
Expression: Warm/confident, soft closed-lip smile, direct eye contact over right shoulder, head turned ~80° back.

Hair: Same color/texture, long, sleek, straight, down back framing right shoulder, soft daylight highlights.
Makeup: Natural daytime (soft base, subtle eyes, neutral matte lip).

Accessories: None.

Outfit: White rib-knit cropped sweater (tight fit) + high-waisted pleated plaid micro-skirt (brown/beige/white), sharp pleats, realistic fabric tension, defined S-curve.

Pose: Torso facing away (~90°), head turned back (~80°), right hand lightly lifting skirt hem, realistic finger/textile interaction.

Mood/Color: Warm, confident travel aesthetic; palette (white, coffee-brown, beige, sky blue), 5500K daylight, medium-high contrast.

Environment: Outdoor terrace, diffused sunlight (upper-right), glass railings + metal posts, distant hills + urban skyline, bright sky w/ soft clouds, stone/concrete detail, background softly blurred (f/2.8).
```

{
  "aspect_ratio": "9:16",

Framed to show her full face and upper shoulders clearly in the mirror reflection, looking straight at her reflection in the mirror with a neutral/relaxed expression, slight foam at the corners of mouth from toothpaste. Mouth slightly open during brushing motion, toothbrush angled naturally against teeth. Shot from the viewer's perspective as if standing behind her looking into the mirror (mirror reflection composition), eye-level framing. Bright even bathroom lighting from overhead LED fixtures (cool-white 4000-5000K) plus soft warm vanity lights around the mirror edges, creating realistic skin highlights, subtle shadows under chin, natural eye catchlights, authentic wet-look shine on lips/teeth from toothpaste/foam. Medium-length chestnut brown hair loosely tied back in a messy low ponytail or bun, a few loose strands framing face. Wearing a simple light gray cotton t-shirt or basic crew-neck top, fully clothed, modest coverage. Delicate fine-line tattoo visible on right forearm if present in reference. Foreground details: modern white porcelain sink with chrome faucet/tap running slightly (small stream of water visible), toothbrush charger on counter, tube of white toothpaste open nearby, marble or white tiled countertop with minor water spots for realism. Background softly blurred: tiled bathroom wall in light neutral tones, shower curtain or glass door faintly visible behind. Ultra-sharp focus on face/reflection, hyper-photorealistic skin details (visible pores, subtle redness, natural texture, light oil sheen on forehead), candid everyday smartphone or point-and-shoot photo aesthetic, natural color grading, high dynamic range, no artificial glow, no over-sharpening, believable depth of field with creamy bokeh on background.",

Her cheeks have subtle contouring with a light peach blush, and her expression is calm, neutral, and soft-glam.

Her hair is extremely long, straight blonde hair, center-parted, flowing behind her shoulders with subtle sun-kissed highlights.

She is wearing a matching two-piece athletic loungewear set in a {argument name="outfit color" default="peach-orange"} color with a subtle lighter geometric/chevron pattern. The outfit includes a short-sleeved scoop-neck crop top and high-waisted leggings extending over the feet like hosiery, featuring a distinct open-thigh cutout on the left leg. She holds a small matte black nylon gym bag in her right hand and a smartphone with a light pink and white floral/damask patterned case.

The setting is a modern, brightly lit indoor bedroom or dressing hallway with light oak wood flooring, clean white walls, and white paneled doors with brass handles. In the background, there is a partially visible bed with white linens and a navy blue throw blanket.

Lighting is bright, soft, and diffused natural light from a side window, creating minimal shadows. The image is high-resolution mobile photography with sharp focus on the subject and slight background blur, featuring warm, vibrant tones and clean whites.

A highly realistic smartphone selfie photo of a Caucasian woman in her mid-20s, captured in a vertical 4:5 aspect ratio. The camera is held at a slightly high angle with an arm-extended perspective, using a wide-angle smartphone lens (around 24–28mm). The framing shows her from head to mid-torso, with sharp focus on her face and a slightly softer background.

She has fair-light skin with warm-neutral undertones, smooth natural texture, and a subtle glow from soft daylight. Her cheeks have a gentle pink flush. Her face is oval-shaped with a soft jawline, medium forehead, slightly full cheeks, and a rounded chin.

Her eyes are light green with hints of hazel, almond-shaped, with long defined lashes and mascara. Her eyebrows are light brown, medium thickness, softly arched, and well-groomed. She has a straight nose with a slightly rounded tip, proportional in size. Her lips are full (especially the lower lip), natural pink in color, with a slightly glossy hydrated look. She is smiling naturally with visible straight white teeth, giving a friendly, confident, relaxed expression, with slightly squinting eyes due to the smile.

Her hair is shoulder-length, dark blonde with lighter blonde highlights (balayage), styled in soft waves with a center part, naturally framing her face.

She has a slim to average build, visible from head to mid-torso, with a relaxed posture slightly angled toward the camera.

She is wearing a fitted white short-sleeve t-shirt made of soft cotton, slightly stretchy, tightly fitted. The shirt has bold red block serif text in all caps on the chest reading: “YES THEY ARE REAL”. She also wears a thin gold chain necklace with a small subtle pendant.

The lighting is natural daylight coming from the front-left, soft and diffused, creating even illumination on her face with gentle shadows and natural highlights.

The background is an indoor home setting, likely a kitchen/living area. Visible elements include a light-colored kitchen counter, white modern bar stools, a dark refrigerator (black or stainless steel) with small items or magnets attached, muted green/gray walls, light brown wooden door trim, and medium warm brown wooden flooring. The background has moderate depth and is slightly blurred.

The overall color palette includes white, red, natural skin tones, green-gray walls, wood browns, and dark appliance tones. The image style is highly realistic with minimal editing, high clarity, low noise, and natural contrast.

## Late Night Bedroom Selfie AI Photo Series

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
I. Subject Setting
1 Character Description: Based on the reference image, a young woman with delicate features, a small and soft face shape, overall makeup referencing the style of a Korean actress. The image should present a natural and playful feeling of a casual late-night bedroom selfie.
2 Age Status: Young adult.

II. Expression and Face
1 Eye State: Soft and focused eyes, with a slight smile, gentle, calm, slightly playful temperament, gaze directed towards the phone screen and lens area.
2 Mouth State: Mouth closed, with a slight arc of a smile, feeling sweet yet restrained, relaxed and natural.
3 Overall Expression: Presenting an intimate, soft, and slightly playful expression, with a subtle and attractive temperament.
4 Facial Retention: Face remains small and natural, cheeks have a soft fleshy feel, jawline is soft, retaining natural asymmetry and realistic proportions.
5 Makeup Characteristics: Korean actress-style makeup, clear and clean base makeup, smooth skin texture, straight and soft eyebrows, light brown eyeliner, natural eyelashes, slight aegyo-sal (under-eye bags), soft blush, delicate nose highlight, low-saturation rose glossy lip color.

III. Hairstyle Setting
1 Hair Color: Deep black.
2 Hair State: Long hair naturally draped, with soft layers and light, airy bangs covering the forehead.
3 Hair Effect: Smooth hair texture, slightly messy, naturally falling around the cheeks and pillow.

IV. Body and Skin Texture
1 Body Characteristics: Slender, compact figure, soft and feminine temperament.
2 Waist State: Waist is slender, but mostly obscured by the curled posture.
3 Chest State: Minimal expression, mostly covered by the posture and phone position.
4 Leg State: The legs are the main subject of the picture, significantly affected by the close-up phone perspective. One knee is close to the lens, and the feet are raised upwards, forming an asymmetrical yet playful arrangement.
5 Exposed Areas: Face, arms, thighs, knees, calves, feet.
6 Skin Tone Characteristics: Fair skin tone, with a soft neutral-to-warm undertone.
7 Skin Texture: Smooth skin, realistically tactile, appearing soft under the flash, avoid plastic smoothing, retain realistic skin details.
8 Light Effect: Direct flash causes bright highlights on the knees, feet, cheeks, and forehead, while shadows naturally transition to the darker areas of the room.

V. Pose and Composition Action
1 Basic Pose: The person is lying on the bed against the wall, upper body slightly propped up, holding a phone in one hand, close to the face.
2 Core Posture: Overall is a natural, curled, casual lying posture.
3 Action Feeling: Legs are raised high, forming a deliberately unbalanced but playful composition, like a casual private snapshot.

VI. Clothing and Accessories
1 Top Setting: Simple black home wear top or dress, black color, minimalist and form-fitting style, mostly obscured due to posture and legs.
2 Top Function: Visual focus is concentrated on the face, legs, and skin illuminated by the flash.
3 Bottom Setting: Bottoms are not clearly visible, dark color or obscured, mostly covered by body posture, bedding, and framing.
4 Phone Setting: Light gold phone, rear flash directly hitting the lens, forming obvious starburst flares.
5 Other Accessories: Beige ribbed knit leg warmers, loosely piled around the calves and ankles.

VII. Shooting Method
1 Overall Style: Realistic phone snapshot texture, close to daily social media photos, with a natural bedroom selfie atmosphere.
2 Camera Angle: Low angle, close to the bed surface, using a wide-angle phone perspective.
3 Image Type: Vertical composition, full-body casual selfie, foreground bedding takes up a significant proportion, exaggerated leg perspective.
4 Aspect Ratio: Vertical long aspect ratio.
5 Image Texture: Phone imaging details are clear, with slight natural noise, flash has realistic bloom, skin is clean but not overly retouched.
6 Lighting Setting: Main light source is direct front flash from the phone, a hard front light. The room is generally dark, and shadows decay quickly.
7 Focus Performance: The phone's depth of field is relatively deep, and most areas of the subject are clearly readable.

VIII. Background Environment
1 Scene Location: A corner of a quiet bedroom at night.
2 Wall Color: Pure, low-saturation gray wall.
3 Background Elements: Foreground has checkered quilt and messy bedding, dark pillow behind the person, white door edge on the left, wooden drawer cabinet on the right.
4 Environment Atmosphere: Private, quiet, slightly lived-in, not overly tidy.
5 Background Lighting: The environment itself is dark, mostly suppressed by the phone flash.

IX. Overall Atmosphere
1 Temperament Keywords: Soft, playful, low-key, unintentional.
2 Emotional State: Intimacy in the deep night, with a slight, gentle smile.
3 Aesthetic Direction: Korean female celebrity aesthetic combined with a realistic bedroom snapshot feel.
4 Realism Requirements: Retain imperfect composition, messy bedding, wide-angle distortion, and harshness of the flash, making the image look more like a real photo rather than a deliberate pose.
5 Atmosphere Expression: Like the room has quieted down, captured in a moment of pause before sleep, with only the phone flash breaking the darkness.
6 Image Story: Not a studio shoot, not a deliberate performance, but capturing a brief and beautiful moment on the bed, where comfort, youth, and natural charm accidentally bring out a cinematic feel.
7 Image Feeling: Must be casual enough to seem real, yet beautiful enough to maintain a sense of distance.
```

## Candid Mirror Selfie with Visible Thong Waistband Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A candid, realistic mirror selfie photograph taken in a brightly lit bedroom during daytime. The image captures a young adult woman from the waist up looking directly at her reflection while taking the photo with her phone. The mood is casual and confident.

She has long blonde hair with a natural wavy texture, parted slightly to the left and falling over her shoulders. She has fair skin with natural texture and a subtle flush on the cheeks. Her blue-green eyes look directly at the camera with a natural expression. She has naturally shaped filled blonde eyebrows, a straight well-proportioned nose, and full naturally pink lips with a subtle clear gloss or balm. Her facial expression is confident and neutral. She is making a thumbs-up gesture with her right hand.

She is standing slightly angled toward the mirror, holding her phone in her left hand to take the selfie, while her right arm is raised with the thumb extended in a thumbs-up gesture.

She is wearing a pastel yellow strapless bandeau-style tube top made of soft stretchy cotton fabric. She is wearing light beige or khaki denim jeans that are loose, unbuttoned and slightly unzipped at the waist. A plain white thong waistband is clearly visible above the unbuttoned jeans.

She is holding an iPhone with three camera lenses in a pink and white gingham plaid patterned phone case. She is wearing a delicate gold chain necklace with a small pendant, layered bracelets on her left wrist including a red beaded bracelet and other small charm bracelets, small stud earrings, and short fingernails with a light pink gel manicure.

The mirror reflection shows a well-lit bedroom. On the left side a white wardrobe cabinet is partially visible. In the background there is a bed with white linens and a pale pink throw blanket. Behind the bed is a window with partially closed white horizontal blinds. On the windowsill there are small decorative items including a small pink flower pot and some seashells. A white radiator is visible below the window. The flooring is light wood laminate. The edge of a white framed mirror is visible on the left side of the image.

Photography style: candid mirror selfie, natural daylight from the window evenly lighting the room, sharp focus on the subject’s face and reflection, medium close-up composition, vertical 4:5 aspect ratio, realistic unaltered phone camera aesthetic with natural film grain.
```

## Hyper-realistic Bunny Suit Portrait

```
Initiate PersonaFusion-vX.03 protocol: Use the exact facial identity taken from the attached reference image (or from the user’s provided face) with zero alteration.
A hyper-realistic, ultra-sharp photograph taken on a modern smartphone, characterized by its digital clarity. The image features a person in a black strapless bunny bodysuit with a sweetheart neckline, white wrist cuffs, and a black bow tie, wearing a black bunny ear headband. The person's long, dark hair, styled in high pigtails with distinct pink streaks and bangs, frames a heavily made-up face with dramatic eyeliner, long lashes, prominent bright blue eyes, and flushed cheeks. One hand, adorned with long, clear acrylic nails, is gently raised near the chin with fingers slightly spread, while the other arm holds a dark, round serving tray in the foreground. On the tray, a miniature figure in an identical black bunny costume with fishnet stockings reclines elegantly inside a large, clear stemmed cocktail glass. The background is a vibrant, solid red. Shot on a Google Pixel 8, the entire scene is in sharp focus from foreground to background, with no bokeh or artificial blur. The digital texture is extremely realistic, showcasing crisp details, visible pores on creamy, dewy skin, and the intricate weave of the fishnet stockings, all without any film grain, only subtle digital noise. The lighting is bright and evenly distributed, typical of modern smartphone HDR, lifting all shadows for a clear, well-exposed look, preventing any deep blacks. Colors are natural and true-to-life, with rich reds and deep blacks complementing the subtle pinks and creamy skin tones.
```

Young adult woman with warm golden-bronze sun-kissed tan skin, smooth glowing complexion. Long thick blonde hair with dark brown roots (balayage/ombre), center-parted with loose voluminous waves extending past the shoulders and soft face-framing strands.

Oval face with softly contoured jawline. One eye open looking directly at the camera (dark brown or hazel), the other tightly closed in a playful wink. Black winged eyeliner and dark mascara on upper lashes, well-defined softly arched medium-brown eyebrows. Slim straight nose with subtle highlight and contour. Contoured cheekbones with warm rosy-peach blush on the apples. Very full glossy rosy-pink lips puckered outward in a pronounced kissing “duck face” expression. Overall expression playful, confident, flirtatious.

She wears a tight light blue and white gingham corset-style camisole with sweetheart neckline, structured underwire cup stitching, thick matching straps, and delicate white scalloped lace trim along the neckline and bottom hem. Bottoms are light-wash denim jeans, low-to-mid rise with the top metal button undone and waistband slightly folded open.

Hands rest lightly on the waist/hips with fingers spread across the ribs. Multiple stacked silver rings on the right hand. Long almond-shaped acrylic nails painted light pink/nude.

Background: modern bright indoor room (bedroom). Smooth white walls and light oak wood flooring. On the left wall a framed pastoral landscape painting of a green field, with the edge of a bed showing white sheets and a textured beige/cream throw blanket. On the right side a dark doorframe or open door and a woven wicker/rattan floor basket partially visible. Ceiling includes a rectangular white HVAC vent and a circular white smoke detector or light fixture.

Photorealistic, high detail, natural indoor portrait.
```

## Photorealistic Selfie of a Young Woman in a Bikini

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Photorealistic selfie of a young adult female in her early 20s, shot in a 3:4 aspect ratio from a high-angle, elevated top-down perspective, using a wide-angle front-facing smartphone lens.

She has an oval face with a defined jawline and pointed chin, fair to light tan skin with warm golden undertones, smooth flawless makeup base, and luminous highlighter on cheekbones, tip of the nose, and forehead.

Her eyes are dark brown or hazel, almond shaped, with very long thick black eyelashes (extensions or heavy mascara), subtle dark eyeliner on the upper lash line, and medium-thick arched medium-brown eyebrows.

Her nose has a straight narrow bridge with a slightly rounded tip, subtle contour on the sides, and a bright highlight down the center and tip.

She has prominent high cheekbones with warm rosy-pink blush on the apples of the cheeks blended upward.

Her lips are plump and full with glossy nude-pink tint, showing straight white upper teeth in a wide smile, with a textured pink tongue playfully sticking out over the bottom lip.

Her hair is bright blonde with darker brown roots, straight with slight volume, parted slightly off-center, falling past the shoulders and framing the face.

Expression is joyful, playful, cheeky, relaxed, eyes slightly crinkled from smiling.

She is standing and leaning slightly forward toward the camera, right arm raised making a peace/V sign, left arm extended forward holding the camera, head tilted slightly upward looking at the lens.

She is wearing a two-piece bikini (bikini top and matching bikini bottoms), athletic fit, revealing midriff.

Footwear: white ankle socks with a faint black logo and brown slip-on slide sandals with mirrored white text reading “eòlhC.”

Accessories include small thick gold hoop earrings, a thin gold chain bracelet with small star/clover charms on the right wrist, and neatly filed nails painted pale pink or off-white.

The setting is an indoor bedroom. On the left side, an unmade bed with a light beige fabric base, crumpled white sheets, two stacked white pillows, and a piece of black clothing on the bed.

On the right side, an open bright pink suitcase on the floor containing folded black, white, and blue clothes, the top of an olive green suitcase near the bottom right, and a dark wooden chair or table leg behind the pink suitcase.

The floor is light oak wood laminate with vertical planks.

Lighting comes from bright natural sunlight streaming from the left, creating strong highlights on the face, hair, and shoulders and crisp shadows on the bed, sheets, and floor, indicating a strong direct light source.
```

Subject centered, kneeling on a bed, occupying ~60% of frame height. Rumpled white bedding fills lower frame; neutral grey wall upper frame. Camera ~1m from mirror (arm’s length).

Identity
Identity Lock: inherit all facial features, skin tone, and hair from the provided selfie. Maintain natural skin texture (pores, fine facial hair, realistic skin physics). Subtle photogenic enhancement: even skin tone, soft jaw contour, gentle cheek highlight, bright natural eyes with catchlights, groomed brows, defined lashes, clean natural teeth.

Expression: relaxed and pensive. Eyes looking at phone screen (~15° off-camera left). Lips closed with soft neutral pout. Brows relaxed.

Hair
Same color and texture as selfie. Long straight hair with messy “bedhead” volume, strands falling over the right shoulder. Soft window light highlights.

Makeup
Winged black eyeliner, light natural foundation, soft rose lip gloss.

Accessories
Light pink smartphone in right hand with black charging cable. Thin black elastic on right wrist. Single white wireless earbud in left ear.

Outfit
Oversized optic-white cotton crew-neck t-shirt, mid-thigh length. Realistic heavyweight fabric wrinkles and drape. Kneeling posture creates natural bunching around waist and hips. Silhouette subtly visible through fabric.

Pose
Kneeling upright on bed, knees together. Torso facing mirror. Head slightly tilted down toward phone. Right hand holding phone, left hand resting behind on mattress.

Mood & Color
Cozy, dreamy mood. Palette: white, slate grey, soft pink, cool daylight blue.

Lighting
Mid-morning window light from the left (~6000K). Soft diffused light wrapping face and body, moderate-high contrast with gentle shadows to the right.

Environment
Bedroom scene: rumpled white duvet and pillows with visible texture. On bed behind subject: pink plush cat-ear headband and pink handheld gaming console. Neutral grey wall background. Small white vanity with pink items partially visible on far left.
```

## High-Fidelity Photographic Transformation Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a high-fidelity photographic transformation. Maintain the exact facial identity from the provided source image with absolute precision and zero alteration.
[SCENE]: A bright, warm-toned indoor setting, likely a home environment. The background is a soft, light-colored wall with abstract patterns of natural light and shadows, possibly from a window or nearby foliage, adding subtle depth. A glimpse of a blurred green plant or object is visible in the far right corner. The composition is an upper-body shot, captured in a portrait orientation, with the subject leaning slightly into the left side of the frame as she holds her phone. The entire scene is in sharp focus, without any background blur or bokeh.
[SUBJECT]: An adult woman with a fair complexion, captured mid-selfie. Her dark brown hair is long and voluminous, styled with soft mermaid waves, prominent butterfly-style layers that frame her face and shoulders, and soft curtain bangs gently sweeping across her forehead. She is wearing a light pink button-up cardigan over a light grey ribbed knit bralette or top featuring a subtle cut-out and delicate strappy detail at the chest. Her right arm is slightly raised, holding a light purple smartphone with a triple camera array towards the camera, while her left shoulder is subtly lower, demonstrating natural weight shift. Her head is slightly tilted to her right (viewer's left), and her gaze is soft and direct, with subtle, natural-looking hazel contact lenses that blend with the natural eye color, adding warmth and depth. Her expression is serene and inviting, with a gentle, soft smile causing a slight micro-tightening at the outer corners of her eyes. Her lips are glossy, plump, and a natural pinkish-mauve with a subtle shimmer, slightly parted. Her brows are relaxed. Her skin is flawless and luminous, featuring a 'glass skin' texture with intense, dewy highlights on the bridge and tip of the nose, complemented by a subtle blush applied with high placement on the cheekbones.
[LIGHTING]: Natural daylight with soft rosy undertones reflecting subtly on the skin, enhancing the natural blush tones. The clean white balance ensures true-to-life colors, with a soft highlight rolloff that prevents harshness.
[AESTHETIC]: A hyper-realistic, ultra-sharp photograph taken on a modern smartphone, specifically an iPhone 15 Pro, characterized by its digital clarity. The image showcases realistic, digital texture with sharp details, including visible pores on the skin. There is minimal contrast, no HDR flattening, and no heavy color grading, preserving the soft pink warmth of the skin. ar 4:5
```

Scene: Subject fills ~65% of frame in crouching/kneeling pose. Upper 40% shows pitch-black night sky and dark marina water; lower 20% textured stone tile floor. Camera ~1.5m above subject angled downward.

Identity Lock: Inherit ALL facial features from the provided selfie (face shape, bone structure, eyes, nose, lips, skin tone, hair).

Photogenic Enhancements: Natural skin clarity with preserved texture, even tone, subtle luminosity, refined jawline shadows, gentle nasal highlight, soft cheekbone highlights, enhanced iris catchlights, natural lash separation, groomed brows, pearl-white teeth, salon-finish hair with controlled flyaways. Bio-fidelity with micro-pores, hydrated T-zone, faint vellus hair.

Expression: Pensive neutral look. Gaze ~45° to mid-right off-camera. Slightly parted lips, relaxed brows, head tilted slightly right.

Hair: Inherit color/texture from selfie. Long sleek straight hair with center part falling behind shoulders. Strong flash highlights creating strand separation.

Makeup: Glossy pink lips, clean foundation, winged black eyeliner, defined lashes, soft blush.

Accessories: Thin gold chain necklace with small heart pendant, crystal-studded silver strap across left shoulder, gold bracelets/watch on left wrist.

Outfit: Light grey rib-knit off-shoulder mini dress with long sleeves. White floral-lace tights. Dark slate-grey suede thigh-high boots. Realistic fabric tension and elbow/waist bunching; detailed lace skin interaction. Defined S-curve silhouette with enhanced waist–hip and leg geometry.

Pose: Torso and hips angled ~45° right. Head turned further right, looking off-camera. Low crouch on stone floor, arms relaxed forward with natural finger articulation.

Mood: Moody urban nighttime aesthetic. Palette: slate grey, optic white, onyx black, pale gold.

Lighting: Harsh direct flash (~6500K) from camera, bright skin highlights and deep shadows. High-contrast flash photography look.

Environment: Night marina/waterfront — dark water with vertical dock light reflections, blurred white yacht mid-left, large textured grey stone tiles underfoot. Sharp subject shadows anchor to the ground.
```

  "master_prompt": {

        "match density, length, shape, and edges",
        "preserve natural tonal variation including salt-and-pepper if visible",
        "beard lines clean and structured"
      ],
      "clean_shaven_rule": "if clean-shaven, emphasize natural jawline definition and skin texture"
    },

    "hairstyle": {
      "adaptive": true,
      "requirement": "match hairstyle from reference image exactly",
      "details": [
        "same volume",
        "same direction",
        "same hairline",
        "natural strand texture",
        "subtle specular highlights from overhead lighting"
      ]
    },

    "pose": {

        "one hand raised to chin",
        "thumb along the jawline",
        "index finger slightly curled",
        "knuckles and tendons subtly defined",
        "hand in sharp focus with natural skin texture"
      ]
    },

    "lighting": {
      "style": "low-key fine-art studio lighting",
      "contrast": "high contrast but refined tonal control",

      "key_light": {

      "highlight_focus": [

        "nose bridge",
        "upper cheekbones",
        "beard texture",

      ],

      ],

      "fill_light": {

      },

      "rim_light": {

Eyebrows: Straight,tebal  natural, soft,.

Blush: Baby pink "over-blush" spreading across the cheeks and bridge of the nose.
Nose & Lips: Small slim nose with soft contour; "cherry pink" glossy jelly lips with a gradient (darker on the inside); well-defined cupid's bow.

Bagian atasnya sedikit berantakan
Environment & Quality:

Atmosphere: Nighttime.
Lighting: Direct flash focusing on the subject, especially the face, creating a bright and glowing effect.
Technical Quality: HD, 8k high resolution.
Aspect Ratio: 9:16.
Don't change face

Initiate PersonaFusion-vX.03 protocol: Use the exact facial identity taken from the attached reference image (or from the user’s provided face) with zero alteration.
[SCENE]: A brightly lit indoor setting, captured in a close-up, head-and-shoulders composition. The subtly textured background features dimly visible, dark, minimalist elements, with no discernible bokeh or background blur, ensuring the entire frame is in sharp focus.
[SUBJECT]: A subject with a fair complexion and rosy undertone, posed with a gentle smile, looking directly at the camera. Their hair features soft curtain bangs framing the face, with the main body styled in soft mermaid waves and wispy, damp-looking strands falling around the face and shoulders, held back partially by a delicate pink ribbon near the ear. Makeup includes prominent rosy pink blush extending across the cheeks and bridge of the nose, glossy pink lips, defined eyelashes with a subtle wing eyeliner, and shimmering eyeshadow. The subject wears delicate, multi-layered pink beaded necklaces with various charms including hearts and small bows, and elegant pearl and gold stud earrings. Their skin is flawless and luminous, featuring a 'glass skin' texture with intense, dewy highlights on the bridge and tip of the nose.
[LIGHTING]: Natural window daylight illuminates the scene with a slightly warm white balance and soft highlight rolloff. Gentle warm window light enhancing natural blush tones reflects subtly on the skin.
[AESTHETIC]: A hyper-realistic, ultra-sharp photograph taken on a modern smartphone, characterized by its digital clarity, specifically a Google Pixel 8. The entire scene is in sharp focus, showcasing realistic digital textures with discernible skin pores and crisp details on accessories. Minimal HDR flattening and preserved blush saturation are evident, resulting in true-to-life, vibrant colors. ar 4:5
```

## Ultra-Realistic Miniature Clone Studio Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use my uploaded photo as the main and only face reference. My face must remain exactly the same — do NOT change facial features, expression, eye shape, nose, lips, skin tone, age, or identity. No face swap, no beautification, no stylization on the face.
Create an ultra-realistic studio portrait using a miniature clone concept:
Main person:
Sitting on the floor, centered in frame, relaxed pose, calm and confident mood. Facial expression must match the reference exactly.
Miniature clones (same face, same identity): Gemini 
• Two miniatures standing on my open palms (palms facing upward)
• One miniature sitting on my right shoulder
• One miniature pushing my right shoe
• One miniature running in the foreground toward the camera (sense of motion but still sharp and realistic)
• One miniature hugging my left knee from behind
All miniatures must have the exact same face, expression, and identity as the main person.
```

## High Fidelity Face Swap Task Definition

    "primary_goal": "Transfer the face from source image (image1) onto the subject in target image (image2) with seamless, ultra-realistic integration",

      "face_target": "image2",
      "preserve_source_identity": true
    },
    "identity": {

      "match_facial_expression": true,
      "maintain_face_structure": true

## Candid Mirror Selfie with Exaggerated Figure and Cluttered Room

> Afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A vertical 2:3 candid mirror selfie of American Woman [uploaded image], Full Bust, Rambut Hitam panjang bergelombang, standing in front of a large round mirror inside a cluttered, personalized bedroom. She holds a smartphone with a colorful cartoon-patterned case up to take the photo, partially covering her face while looking at the screen. One arm crosses lightly over her midriff, subtly emphasizing her dramatically defined hourglass silhouette with an extremely narrow cinched waist and significantly wide, rounded fitness-style hips and glutes, along with thick toned thighs. She wears a tight black strapless tube top that reveals her midriff and high-waisted vibrant green booty shorts featuring a large smiling Bulbasaur cartoon face printed across the front. A delicate silver necklace with a small pendant rests at her collarbone. The mirror’s circular frame is visible, reflecting the entire messy room: a bed with a pink and white patterned duvet, a pink metal locker-style cabinet covered in stickers, several framed anime posters on the wall, and a floor scattered with clothes, boxes, boots, and miscellaneous items. A doorway appears in the right-side reflection. Natural ambient light mixed with indoor lighting brightens the room evenly, showing realistic colors and textures. Shot on a smartphone with an unedited, spontaneous mirror-selfie aesthetic.
```

      "body_orientation": "The body leaning forward and angled towards the right side of the frame.",
      "hands": "Hands resting on the thighs to support the leaning posture.",
      "overall_dynamic": "A cute, youthful standing pose that looks candid and energetic."
    },
    "composition": {
      "framing": "Medium-full body shot.",
      "visual_balance": "Off-center composition. The subject is positioned in the **left third** of the frame (Rule of Thirds).",
      "directional_flow": "The subject's body and gaze are directed from the left side towards the right (into the negative space).",
      "camera_angle": "Eye-level or slightly low angle to accentuate the legs."
    },
    "lighting": {
      "type": "Direct On-Camera Flash (Night).",
      "characteristics": "Hard, direct flash illuminating the subject, creating sharp contrast against the darker street background.",
      "shadows": "Hard drop shadows falling behind the subject."
    },
    "environment": {

      "atmosphere": "Casual, spontaneous, gritty but stylish."
    },
    "color_palette": {
      "style": "Fujifilm Classic Negative (NC).",
      "tones": "Moody cool tones in the background, warm flash tones on the skin. High contrast."
    },

      "center frame",
      "centered subject",
      "legs apart",
      "wide stance",
      "straight legs",
      "looking left",
      "body facing left",
      "bad anatomy",
      "distorted hands",
      "missing fingers",
      "blurry face",
      "flat lighting"
    ]
  }
}

## Oppenheimer Scene Face Swap and Restoration

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the uploaded Oppenheimer movie frame as the PRIMARY and ONLY scene reference.

Reproduce the scene EXACTLY as shown:
same interior room and background blur,
same soft daylight from camera-left,
same muted green–yellow color temperature,
same camera angle and proximity (tight close-up),
same framing and crop,
same shallow depth of field,
same outfit (teal shirt, dark tie),
same hand-to-temple gesture,
same ring on finger,
same anxious, mentally strained expression,
same posture and head tilt.

DO NOT reinterpret the scene.
DO NOT change the environment, lighting, or composition.

Replace ONLY the face with my uploaded face reference.
My face must remain 100% identical:
exact facial structure,
skin tone,
eye shape,
nose,
lips,
jawline,
hairline,
stubble pattern preserved.

Face swap must perfectly match:
original head angle,
original facial muscle tension,
original eye direction,
original lighting and shadow falloff.

Ultra-realistic cinematic realism.
Natural film grain preserved.
No stylization, no creative liberty.

Aspect ratio: 1.90:1 (IMAX digital frame).
Full headroom preserved.
No crop, no zoom, no reframing.
No background change
No lighting change
No pose change
No hand movement
No expression change
No color grading
No stylization
No AI artifacts
No face morphing
No resemblance to Cillian Murphy
```

The space feels personal. The bokeh is due to the camera's limitations, not artificial depth-of-field effects. The overall atmosphere is warm, bright, and intimate, reminiscent of a real vlog moment just before the subject begins to speak.

The woman's dialogue is: "Today's fashion highlight is [Add fashion features]!" She speaks in a gentle tone while touching her clothes. She smiles at the end.
